Methodology

Historical DataPrevious-season completed ESPN games
Team Rating45% scoring margin, 30% win percentage, 15% offensive production, adjusted 90/10 with opponent strength of schedule
In Season BlendPreseason rating (previous season, regressed 65% toward league average) is blended with this season's to-date rating, gaining ~12% weight per completed game (each team's own game count) up to an 85% cap, so form early in a new season doesn't outweigh a small sample, but a stale preseason read doesn't linger all year either.
Home FieldNFL +1.75 points; college +2.5 points; 0 for neutral-site games
ProbabilityPredicted margin converted through logistic probability
MarketOptional 40% market / 60% independent model blend, averaged (no-vig) across every US book The Odds API returns
AtsAgainst-the-spread mode ranks and picks on cover probability: the model's margin is blended 40% toward the market's implied margin (mirroring the moneyline market blend) before comparing to the consensus sportsbook spread, instead of straight-up win probability. Games without a quoted spread fall back to straight-up for that game and are flagged with ats_warning.
Field ConsensusStraight-up mode blends 15% toward the field's own pick percentage. ATS mode fades it instead - heavy public backing on a side is treated as a mild signal against that side of the number.
LeverageModel win probability minus field ownership
ConfidenceGames ranked by decision score and assigned descending points
InjuriesNFL only: the model's margin moves up to 3 points when a team's primary starting QB (most starts this season) is Out/IR (Doubtful counts 75%), or when he returns after the team's rating was built on backup starts. Other injuries are shown but not adjusted for.
NoteResearch/decision-support model; not a guarantee of outcomes.
Model %/ATS %/spreads are research/decision-support estimates, not a guarantee of outcomes. Spreads are a no-vig consensus average across every US bookmaker The Odds API returned for each game — click "Books" on a card to see each book's individual line. The Predicted ATS cover %/Win % shown on each card is for the team named in parentheses next to it (the picked side), not the home team.
